Step size scaling: Use normalized gradient to get eta O(1). Let's implement mirror step with gradient scaling:
```
g = grad
g_min = g.min(); g_max = g.max(); rng = g_max-g_min
if rng < 1e-30: return no move

# lambda parameter maybe in [0, 2]

```
This makes step independent of gradient scale, but objective line search controls. We can use `lam` instead of eta. Start lam=0.1? Mirror step with normalized gradient:
```
def apply_mirror(p, lp, g, lam):
    # g finite
    g_center = g - g.mean()
    scale = np.max(g_center) - np.min(g_center)
    if scale <= 1e-30:
       return p,lp,0
    step = (g_center / scale) * lam   # range length 1? Actually scale = max-min, so step range lam.
    if lam > 3: clip step to [-1,1]? Maybe.
    lp2 = lp - step
    offset = lp2.max()
    p2 = np.exp(lp2 - offset)
    s = p2.sum()
    if not finite or s<1e-300: return p,lp,0
    p2 /= s
    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-300)
    p2 /= p2.sum()
    lp2 = np.log(p2)
    return p2,lp2,1
```
lm likely 0.1 to 2. If scale tiny, step still uses direction but lam normalizes to fixed range. If gradient direction is noise/small, may move randomly. We can require scale > some tol relative to mean? But if scale tiny, landscape flat; any move small effect on q. Could break or reduce lam. Use scale threshold 1e-12.

      grad, L, q, qmax = ...
      # compute normalized step
      for ls in range(max_ls):
          p2,lp2,ok = apply_mirror(p,lp,grad,lam)
          if not ok: break
          L2, qmax2 = smooth_values(p2,beta)
          if L2 < L - 1e-12 or qmax2 < qmax - 1e-18:
              p,lp=p2,lp2; L,qmax=L2,qmax2
              lam = min(lam*1.05, max_lam)
              improved=True
              break
          lam *= 0.5
      if not improved:
          # try perturb/restart from best
          restart_count +=1
          lam=lam0*0.5? 
          if too many: break
          # perturb p_best
```

def central_x(n):
    x = np.empty(n); x[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n): x[i]=x[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2.0*i)
    return x

```
p = p0.copy(); lp = log(max(p,1e-300))
M = conv_square(p).max()
best_p=p.copy(); best_M=M
stall=0
restart=0
lam=lam0
rng=default_rng(seed)
while time < limit:
    q=conv_square(p); M=q.max()
    if M < best_M - 1e-18: best...
    if stall > max_stall: perturb/restart
    # weights
    scale=gamma*M if gamma>0 else tiny
    z=(q-M)/scale
    z-=z.max() # 0
    e=np.exp(z)
    Z=e.sum()
    w=e/Z
    # optional top active to reduce noise: if sum weights < active_count? no.
    grad = grad_from_weights(p,w) (FFT unless nonneg count small)
    g = grad - grad.mean()
    gscale = g.max()-g.min()
    if gscale < 1e-15: stall++ continue
    # candidate lams
    base_lam = lam
    cands = [base_lam*fact for fact in [0.25,0.5,1,2,4,8]] maybe
    best_trial_p=None; best_trial_M=M
    for l in cands:
       p2,lp2,ok = apply_mirror(lp,g,l)
       if not ok continue
       M2=conv_square(p2).max()
       if M2 < best_trial_M:
           best_trial_M=M2; best_trial_p=p2; best_trial_lp=lp2; best_lam=l
    if best_trial_p is not None and best_trial_M < M - 1e-18:
       p,lp,M=...
       lam = best_lam if best_trial_M < M_old? else lam
       stall=0
       # update local best
    else:
       stall +=1
       if stall % 10 ==0:
          # perturb
          sigma = min(0.2, 0.02* (1+restart*0.5))
          p = perturbed(p,lp,sigma,rng); M=...
          if M > best_M*(1+0.05): revert best
       if stall > max_stall: break/restart from best
```

def apply_mirror(lp, g, lam):
    gc = g - g.mean()
    sc = gc.max()-gc.min()
    if sc <= 1e-30: return None
    step = lam * gc / sc
    # perhaps clip step to [-1,1] to avoid too large

    lp2 = lp - step
    ...

Coordinate polish function:
Use current p and M. We can do a simple move optimization with gradients from active weights.
```
def coordinate_polish(p, M, time_lim, seed):
    p_best=p; M_best=M
    while time...:
       q=conv_square(p); M=q.max()
       # active peaks
       active = np.where(q >= M - 0.05*M)[0] # within 5%
       if len(active)<=300: w uniform active else top 300 by q
       w=1/len(active)
       g=grad_from_weights(p,w)
       # scores c = g/2
       # sources: top 5 c; dests: bottom 5 c
       srcs=np.argsort(c)[-5:][::-1]; dsts=np.argsort(c)[:5]
       improved=False
       for s in srcs:
          if p[s]<=1e-12: continue
          for d in dsts:
             if d==s: continue
             # Try transfer fractions. To keep sum1, p_trial copy.
             # Choose baseline mass m = p[s]
             # fractions [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75] maybe also small [0.02,0.05]
             # To avoid noise, if c[s] <= c[d]+1e-12 continue (moving high gradient to low)
             for frac in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5]:
                 amt=p[s]*frac
                 p2=p.copy(); p2[s]-=amt; p2[d]+=amt; if min<0 continue; normalize
                 M2=conv_square(p2).max()
                 if M2 < M_best - 1e-16:
                    p,p_best=...; M,M_best...; improved=True; break all
       if not improved:
          # random coordinate move or perturb
          if rng... fraction small; accept if improves
          stall++
          if stall>50: perturb p exp noise
```
This is slow due copies. Use preallocated? n=4700, copying for each trial heavy. We can implement using `np.add`? For quick, okay if few trials. Maybe 500 iterations*25 trials*copy 4700 ~59M, okay but FFT dominates.
We can reduce copies by evaluating only best s/d pairs.

Alternative use `p2 = p.copy(); p2[s]-=amt; p2[d]+=amt; p2/=p2.sum()`; no need if sum stays 1 (sum unchanged exactly) unless p2[s] negative. Since transfer preserves sum, no normalization needed. Good! Just p2 sum remains 1. If amt≤p[s], no norm. This saves. Use direct p2.

After move, M2. If improved, accept. Could do more optimal transfer via binary on a chosen pair: function M(amt) maybe unimodal; perform golden search with 6 evals for best pair. But simpler fractions.

def optimize_p_robust(p, time_limit, seed=0, label=''):
    if time_limit <= 0: return p
    p = np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<... return p
    p=p/s
    t0=time.monotonic()
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_p(p)
    # if current not best, initialize best locally
    remaining = time_limit - elapsed
    # Step 1: smooth large beta
    if remaining > 30:
        t1 = min(remaining*0.35, 300)
        p1 = soft_smooth_mirror(p, beta_gamma=0.5, lam0=0.3, time_limit=t1, seed=seed)
        update local best
    # Step 2: smooth medium
    remaining = ...
    t2 = min(remaining*0.35, 250)
    p2 = soft_smooth_mirror(p, beta_gamma=0.1, lam0=0.2, ...)
    # Step3: active sharp
    t3 = min(remaining*0.25, 200)
    p3 = active_mirror(p, gamma=0.03, lam0=0.1, ...)
    # Step4: coord
    t4 = remaining*0.5
    p4 = coord_polish(p, t4,...)
    update best each
    return best_p
```

       if time_inner > ...

       gc=grad - grad.mean(); sc=gc.max()-gc.min()
       if sc < 1e-15:

       else:

          lam_try=lam

Use `apply_mirror` with `gc` already centered but not scaled? We can pass gc and lam, inside scale by sc? We can precompute `g_unit = gc/sc`. Then apply uses `step = lam*g_unit`. Since range =1. Let's do that. If sc small, no move.
`apply_mirror(lp, g_unit, lam)`: step = lam*g_unit; clip? Maybe if lam>1, step in [-0.5lam,0.5lam]. Fine.
```
def apply_mirror(lp, g_unit, lam):
    if lam <=0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(g_unit)): return None,None
    step = lam * np.asarray(g_unit)
    # to avoid huge, clip
    step = np.clip(step, -3.0, 3.0)
    lp2 = lp - step
    off = lp2.max()
    p2 = np.exp(lp2-off)
    s=p2.sum()
    if not finite or s<...: return None,None
    p2/=s
    p2=np.maximum(p2,1e-300); p2/=p2.sum()
    lp2=np.log(p2)
    return p2,lp2
```
Centered g_unit range [-0.5,0.5]. lam=2 gives step ±1, ratio e^1=2.7; lam=5 gives clip ±3 (if no clip) ratio 20; clip at 3 prevents huge. Good.

Coefficient:

with p_i=0 outside. This is exact! Great. We can update q in O(n) per trial to compute max without FFT. This is much faster for coordinate polish and maybe line search? Implement `trial_transfer_max(p, q, s, d, alpha)`:
```
q2 = q.copy()
if alpha !=0:
    # linear part: p array
    # p_{k-d}: for k=d..d+n-1, values p[0..n-1]
    # p_{k-s}: for k=s..s+n-1, p[0..n-1]
    lo_d=d; hi_d=d+n-1; q2[lo_d:hi_d+1] += 2*alpha*p
    lo_s=s; hi_s=s+n-1; q2[lo_s:hi_s+1] -= 2*alpha*p
    # quadratic constants:
    if 2*d <= 2*n-2: q2[2*d] += alpha*alpha
    if s+d <= ...: q2[s+d] -= 2*alpha*alpha
    if 2*s <= ...: q2[2*s] +=? Wait (z^d - z^s)^2 = z^{2d} -2 z^{s+d}+ z^{2s}. Coefficient at 2s: +α^2. Yes.
    # careful if indices coincide: the operations add correctly.
    # Since linear part uses old p, okay.
M2 = q2.max()
```
Need ensure p_{k-d} when k-d within 0..n-1; for k from d to d+n-1. Same for s. If d or s out of range no. This O(n) copy q. For evaluating many trials, copying q (9400) each is okay. Could optimize by using in-place with deltas but not needed.

`next_pow2(m)`:
```

```
For m int.

```

```

`conv_square(p)`:
```

A=np.fft.rfft(p, fl)

return B[:2*n-1]
```
If p contains tiny negative due to ops? Use p=np.maximum? In line search may have negative? We ensure p nonnegative. But for safety in conv_square, `p = np.maximum(p,0.0)`? This allocation. `soft_values(p,beta)`:
```

M=float(q.max())
if beta <= 1e-300: beta=1e-300
z=(q-M)/beta
e=np.exp(z)

if Z<=0 or not finite: w=np.zeros_like(q); w[argmax]=1; Z=1
else: w=e/Z
F = M + beta*np.log(Z)
return F,M,w,q
```
Since z<=0, e finite. If beta small and many maxima, Z count, F M+small*log.

`weighted_gradient(p,w)`:
```

if cnt <= 128:

   g=np.zeros(n)

      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)

M=fft_len_wt(n)

```

`coord_polish` exact transfers:
```
def coord_polish(p0, time_limit, seed=0, label=''):
    p=p0.copy(); q=conv_square(p); M=q.max(); best...
    rng=...
    t0=monotonic; stall=0; recompute_count=0
    while time:
       # perhaps recompute q every 100 iterations to avoid drift
       recompute_count+=1
       if recompute_count % 50 ==0:
           p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); p/=s; q=conv_square(p); M=q.max()
           if M<best_M update
       T=min(40,2*n-1)
       top=np.argsort(q)[::-1][:T]
       w=np.zeros(2*n-1); w[top]=1.0/T
       h = weighted_gradient(p,w)/2  # direct likely
       # if h has negative tiny, max
       srcs=np.argsort(h)[-4:][::-1]
       dsts=np.argsort(h)[:4]
       improved=False
       for s_idx in srcs:
          pi=p[s_idx]
          if pi <= 1e-15: continue
          # Try dests
          for d_idx in dsts:
             if d_idx == s_idx: continue
             if h[s_idx] <= h[d_idx] + 1e-15: continue
             # Max alpha pi. Try fractions
             best_local_M = M
             best_local_alpha = None; best_q2=None
             # fractions: small to large
             for frac in (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6):
                 alpha=frac*pi
                 q2=q.copy()
                 # linear updates using old p
                 # d linear: q[d:d+n] += 2*alpha*p
                 q2[d_idx:d_idx+n] += 2.0*alpha*p
                 q2[s_idx:s_idx+n] -= 2.0*alpha*p
                 # quadratic
                 if 2*d_idx <= 2*n-2: q2[2*d_idx] += alpha*alpha
                 if s_idx+d_idx <= 2*n-2: q2[s_idx+d_idx] -= 2.0*alpha*alpha
                 if 2*s_idx <= 2*n-2: q2[2*s_idx] += alpha*alpha
                 M2=float(np.max(q2))
                 if M2 < best_local_M:
                     best_local_M=M2; best_local_alpha=alpha; best_q2=q2; best_trial_p? store
             # If best_local_M < M - tol: accept
             if best_local_alpha is not None and best_local_M < M - 1e-16:
                 p2=p.copy(); p2[s_idx]-=best_local_alpha; p2[d_idx]+=best_local_alpha
                 p2 = np.maximum(p2,0) # just in case
                 # sum preserved; normalize tiny
                 pp=p2.sum(); if pp>0: p2/=pp
                 p=p2; q=best_q2; M=best_local_M
                 best update improved=True; break
          if improved: break
       if not improved:
          stall +=1
          if stall % 5 ==0:
             # random transfer high->low
             s_idx=int(rng.integers(n)); d_idx=int(rng.integers(n))
             if d_idx==s_idx or p[s_idx]<=1e-15: stall continue
             if h[s_idx] > h[d_idx]:
                alpha=rng.uniform(0.01,0.05)*p[s_idx]
                evaluate; if improve accept
          if stall > 100:
             # perturb

```
import numpy as np
import time

_BEST = {'seq': None, 'score': float('inf')}
_FFT_CACHE = {}

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()

def _fft_len_sq(n):
    key = ('sq', n)
    v = _FFT_CACHE.get(key)
    if v is None:
        v = _next_pow2(2*n - 1)
        _FFT_CACHE[key] = v
    return v
...
```
For `n` maybe numpy int? Cast int.

`normalize(p)`:
```
def normalize(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300: return np.ones_like(p)/p.size
   return p/s
```

`score_sequence(p)`:
```
def score_sequence(p):
   p=np.asarray... maybe normalize robust
   s=p.sum(); if s<=... return inf
   x=p/s
   q=conv_square(x)
   return float(2.0*len(x)*q.max())
```
This normalizes and calls conv. For p already normalized, okay.

def weighted_gradient(p,w):
    n=int(p.size)
    L=2*n-1
    # if w length maybe L
    if w.size != L:
       if w.size < L: w=np.pad...
       else: w=w[:L]
    mask = w > 1e-14
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if cnt <= 128:
        active = np.flatnonzero(mask)
        for k in active:
            wi = float(w[k])
            if wi <= 0: continue
            lo = k - n + 1
            if lo < 0: lo = 0
            hi = k
            if hi > n - 1: hi = n - 1
            if lo > hi: continue
            idx = np.arange(lo, hi+1)
            g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wi*p[k-hi:k-lo-1:-1]? Simpler idx.
    else:
        M = fft_len_wt(n)
        R = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M)
        W = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
        h = np.fft.irfft(R*W, M)
        h = h[n-1:2*n-1]
        np.maximum(h,0.0,out=h)
        return 2.0*h
```

src = k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)
g[lo:hi+1] += 2*wi*p[src]
```

`soft_values`:
```
def soft_values(p,beta,need_weights=True):
    q=conv_square(p)
    M=float(q.max())
    if beta <= 1e-300:
       beta=1e-300
    z=(q-M)/beta
    e=np.exp(z)
    Z=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
       Z=1.0
       e=np.zeros_like(q); e[int(np.argmax(q))]=1.0
    F = M + beta*float(np.log(Z))
    if need_weights:
       w=e/Z
       return F,M,w,q
    return F,M,None,q
```
Since z max 0, e finite. If beta tiny and q differences large, underflow to 0 for non-max; fine.

`active_weights(q,gammaM)` maybe separate:
```
def make_weights(q,gamma):
   M=float(q.max()); beta=gamma*M; if beta<=0: top?
   z=(q-M)/beta; e=exp(z); Z=e.sum(); w=e/Z; return w,M
```
For active, if gamma very small, maybe use top 100 to focus. Can use same with gamma=0.02. If M=0, no.
If beta=0 due to M=0, invalid (sum positive) no.

Pseudo `soft_smooth_mirror` complete:
```
def soft_smooth_mirror(p0,beta_gamma=0.1,lam0=0.2,time_limit=60.0,seed=0,label=''):
    p=normalize(p0)
    n=p.size
    t0=time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic()-t0 >= time_limit: return p
    lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
    q0=conv_square(p); M0=float(q0.max())
    beta=max(1e-300, beta_gamma*M0)
    F,M,w,q = soft_values(p,beta,True)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_M=M; best_F=F
    lam=lam0; stall=0; restart=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        # if diverged, reset
        if M > best_M*(1.10) and stall > 20:
            p=best_p.copy(); lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); F,M,w,q=soft_values(p,beta,True); continue
        g=weighted_gradient(p,w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            p=best_p.copy(); ...; continue
        gc=g-g.mean()
        sc=float(gc.max()-gc.min())
        if sc < 1e-14:
            # perturb
            restart +=1
            if restart > 40: break
            sigma=0.03 + 0.01*min(restart,10)
            p=perturb_lowfreq(p,sigma,3+restart%5,rng)
            lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300))
            F,M,w,q=soft_values(p,beta,True)
            if M < best_M: best...
            stall=0
            continue
        g_unit=gc/sc
        accepted=False
        # candidate list based on lam
        cands=lam
        lam_list=[]
        lam_list.append(lam)
        if lam > 5e-3: lam_list.append(0.5*lam)
        if lam > 1e-3: lam_list.append(0.25*lam)
        lam_list.append(2.0*lam)
        lam_list.append(4.0*lam)
        lam_list.append(0.1*lam)
        # sort by closeness? Keep order.
        for l in lam_list:
            if l <= 1e-6: continue
            p2,lp2=apply_mirror(lp,g_unit,l)
            if p2 is None: continue
            F2,M2,w2,q2=soft_values(p2,beta,False)
            if np.isfinite(F2) and F2 < F - 1e-14:
                allow = max(M*(1.25), best_M*(1.03))

    return best_p

```
def perturb_lowfreq(p,sigma,n_modes=3,rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    n=p.size
    if n<=1: return p.copy()
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    phi=np.zeros(n)
    for j in range(1,int(n_modes)+1):
        a=float(rng.standard_normal()); b=float(rng.standard_normal())
        if j == 1:
           phi += a*np.cos(np.pi*t) + b*np.sin(np.pi*t)
        else:
           phi += a*np.cos(2*np.pi*j*t) + b*np.sin(2*np.pi*j*t)
    phi -= phi.mean()
    sd=phi.std()
    if sd > 1e-12: phi *= (1.0/sd)
    lp=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)) + sigma*phi
    off=lp.max(); p2=np.exp(lp-off); s=p2.sum(); if s<=... return p.copy; p2/=s; return p2
```
This uses `t` each call allocation; okay. Could use global arange but n varies. For many perturbations, allocation maybe. Accept.

def optimize_p_robust(p0, time_limit, seed=0, label=''):
    p=normalize(p0)
    if time_limit <= 5: return p
    t0=monotonic; best=p; best_sc=score_normalized? # use score_sequence
    rem = time_limit - elapsed
    if rem > 20:
       t1 = min(rem*0.35, 300.0)
       p1 = soft_smooth_mirror(p, beta_gamma=0.6, lam0=0.3, time_limit=t1, seed=seed+1, label=label+'S1')
       sc1=score_sequence(p1)
       if sc1 < best_sc: best=p1; best_sc=sc1
    # use best for next
    p=best; rem=...
    if rem >20: t2=min(rem*0.35, 240); p2=soft_smooth_mirror(p,beta_gamma=0.1,lam0=0.25,...); update
    # active
    if rem >15: t3=min(rem*0.30, 200); p3=active_mirror(p,gamma=0.04,lam0=0.1,...)
    # coord
    if rem >10: t4=max(0, min(rem*0.5, 400)); p4=coord_polish(p,t4,...)
    return best
```

But if parent not added via raw? We can add parent to raw first. Then `make_signature` top-level.

`make_signature(p)`:
```
step=max(1,len(p)//30)
vals=np.round(p[::step]*1000.0)
return (len(p), tuple(vals.tolist()))
```
If p has large dynamic, rounding 3 decimals maybe too coarse; okay for dedup.

Now, after initial candidates, multi-length. We need choose new_n based on `n_best = len(_BEST['seq'])`. If best seq None fallback. Candidate deltas. Avoid duplicate same as best. Rem <80 break. For each new_n:
```
p_int=interpolate_seq(_BEST['seq'], new_n)
p_c=make_central_norm(new_n)
# choose lower score
p_st=int; sc=score_sequence(p_int); p_c score; if sc > sc_c: p_st=p_c
if sc < _BEST['score']*1.03:
   t=min(rem*0.3, 240) if large else
   p_opt=optimize_p_robust(p_st,t,seed=...)
```
Maybe if central new_n score better than best by much, optimize. Use `min_score=min(sc,sc_c)`. If min_score < _BEST['score'] + 0.05? Since scores around 1.5, absolute 0.05. Use relative 1.05. Good.
To not spend too much, iterate at most 3 new lengths.

Final polish: If remaining >30, use best sequence:
```
rem=...
t_act=min(rem*0.35,180)
p=active_mirror(_BEST['seq'], t_act,gamma=0.02,lam0=0.05,seed=7)
try_update
rem=...
t_coord=min(rem*0.55,300)
p=coord_polish(_BEST['seq'],t_coord,seed=13)
try_update
```
Could also soft smooth low beta. But active/coord enough.

Fallback: If best None: `x=make_central(4000)`, p=x/sum, try_update. Return.

At end, final official:
```
seq=_BEST['seq']
if seq is None: ...
seq=list(seq)
try: off=evaluate_sequence(seq)
except Exception: off=_BEST['score']
if off != _BEST['score']: print
return seq
```

Need if official returns NaN but fast finite? Return fast best. Good.

Let's code. Beware of `globals().get` inside function: allowed. The rule no IO. fine.

Now, let's review possible syntax around `np.fft.irfft` default return length: If given n? `np.fft.irfft(a, n=M)` returns M real. We call `np.fft.irfft(R*W, M)`. Good.

`conv_square`: `B = np.fft.irfft(A*A, fl)`; for real but A*A may have tiny imaginary; irfft okay. `B[:size]`. Could contain small negative; for max, no issue. But q used in gradient positive? If tiny negative, weights small. For coordinate q copied and updated from approximate q; negative tiny. okay.

Now, let's ensure `weighted_gradient` direct uses `w` length L; if e from soft/exp length L. If `cnt` <=128, active from flatnonzero. For each k, `idx = np.arange(lo, hi+1)`; `g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wi*p[k - hi:k - lo -1:-1]`? Simpler:
```
src = k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)
g[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wi*p[src]
```
This allocates src length; okay. Could use `p[k: k-lo-1:-1]`? if k=n-1, lo=0, `p[n-1::-1]` gives p[n-1]..p[0], matches? For general hi/lo, slice reversed. But using src clear.

In FFT gradient, `h = h[n-1:2*n-1]`; length n. If n=1, slice [0:1]. Good. `np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)` after `return 2.0*h`? Need return nonnegative. Do `np.maximum(h,0.0,out=h); return 2.0*h`. If out h, then return new? `2.0*h` creates. okay.

In `apply_mirror`, `lp` might contain -inf? We maintain log of p>=1e-300; min log ~-690. `lp - step` fine. `lp2.max()` finite. `np.exp(lp2-off)` for very negative underflows 0; okay. `p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-300)` could make sum slightly >; normalize.

In soft/active, if p has many zeros, `lp` floor; gradient maybe zero for those; mirror can re-add mass if grad low.

Now, official `evaluate_sequence` may return np.inf for sum<0.01. Our p sum1, no. If sequence length huge, fast score finite.

Let's include `np.seterr(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore')`? Could avoid warnings. At top after import:
```
np.seterr(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore')
```
Allowed. Good.

Potential issue with `np.exp(z)` where z very negative underflow; underflow ignored. Good.

Now, is there a hidden requirement to include short docstring at top. We'll include.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
- Use a strong analytic initializer based on the coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2)
  (central binomial / 4^i), plus low-frequency deformations of it.
- Optimize with smoothed-max mirror descent (temperature-scheduled), then sharp
  max-subgradient mirror descent, and finally exact-increment coordinate transfer.
- Track the best sequence by a fast FFT convolution objective; final output is
  normalized non-negative heights.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

np.seterr(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore")

_BEST = {"seq": None, "score": float("inf")}
_FFT_CACHE = {}


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_len_sq(n):
    n = int(n)
    key = ("sq", n)
    v = _FFT_CACHE.get(key)
    if v is None:
        v = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        _FFT_CACHE[key] = v
    return v


def _fft_len_wt(n):
    n = int(n)
    key = ("wt", n)
    v = _FFT_CACHE.get(key)
    if v is None:
        v = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
        _FFT_CACHE[key] = v
    return v


def normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones_like(p, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, p.size)
    return p / s


def conv_square(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    fl = _fft_len_sq(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(p, fl)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fl)
    return B[: 2 * n - 1]


def score_normalized(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    q = conv_square(p)
    m = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def score_sequence(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    p = p / s
    return score_normalized(p)


def try_update(p, label=""):
    if p is None:
        return
    p = normalize(p)
    sc = score_normalized(p)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < _BEST["score"] - 1e-14:
        _BEST["score"] = float(sc)
        _BEST["seq"] = p.copy()
        print(f"  ** new best [{label}] score={sc:.8f} n={len(p)}")
    return sc


def make_central(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        x[i] = x[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return x


def make_central_p(n):
    x = make_central(n)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n) / max(1, n)
    return x / s


def add_x_candidate(cands, x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    n = int(x.size)
    if n == 0:
        return
    sx = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sx) or sx <= 1e-300:
        return
    q = conv_square(x)
    m = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return
    sc = float(2.0 * n * m / (sx * sx))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    p = x / sx
    cands.append((sc, len(cands), p))


def make_signature(p):
    n = int(len(p))
    step = max(1, n // 30)
    vals = np.round(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)[::step] * 1000.0)
    return (n, tuple(vals.tolist()))


def perturb_lowfreq(p, sigma, n_modes=3, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return p.copy()
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    phi = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    n_modes = max(1, int(n_modes))
    for j in range(1, n_modes + 1):
        a = float(rng.standard_normal())
        b = float(rng.standard_normal())
        theta = 2.0 * np.pi * float(j) * t
        phi += a * np.cos(theta) + b * np.sin(theta)
    phi -= phi.mean()
    sd = float(phi.std())
    if sd > 1e-12:
        phi = phi / sd
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300)) + float(sigma) * phi
    off = float(lp.max())
    p2 = np.exp(lp - off)
    s = float(p2.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return p.copy()
    p2 = p2 / s
    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-300)
    s2 = float(p2.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        return p.copy()
    return p2 / s2


def weighted_gradient(p, w):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size < L:
        w2 = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w2[: w.size] = w
        w = w2
    elif w.size > L:
        w = w[:L]

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = w > 1e-14
    cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))

    if cnt <= 128:
        active = np.flatnonzero(mask)
        for k in active:
            wi = float(w[k])
            if wi <= 0.0:
                continue
            k = int(k)
            lo = k - n + 1
            if lo < 0:
                lo = 0
            hi = k
            if hi > n - 1:
                hi = n - 1
            if lo > hi:
                continue
            src = k - np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            g[lo : hi + 1] += 2.0 * wi * p[src]
        np.maximum(g, 0.0, out=g)
        return g

    M = _fft_len_wt(n)
    R = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], M)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    h = np.fft.irfft(R * W, M)
    h = np.asarray(h[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1], dtype=np.float64)
    np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)
    return 2.0 * h


def soft_values(p, beta, need_weights=True):
    q = conv_square(p)
    M = float(q.max())
    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-300:
        beta = 1e-300
    z = (q - M) / beta
    e = np.exp(z)
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
        Z = 1.0
        e = np.zeros_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
        e[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
    F = M + beta * float(np.log(Z))
    if need_weights:
        w = e / Z
        return F, M, w, q
    return F, M, None, q


def apply_mirror(lp, g_unit, lam):
    if lam <= 1e-12:
        return None, None
    g_unit = np.asarray(g_unit, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(g_unit)):
        return None, None
    step = float(lam) * g_unit
    # Keep log-scale moves numerically safe.  Since g_unit is centered and has
    # range 1, this limits per-coordinate log multiplicators.
    step = np.clip(step, -2.8, 2.8)
    lp2 = np.asarray(lp, dtype=np.float64) - step
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(lp2)):
        return None, None
    off = float(lp2.max())
    p2 = np.exp(lp2 - off)
    s = float(p2.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None, None
    p2 = p2 / s
    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-300)
    s2 = float(p2.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-300:
        return None, None
    p2 = p2 / s2
    lp2 = np.log(p2)
    return p2, lp2


def soft_smooth_mirror(p0, beta_gamma=0.1, lam0=0.2, time_limit=60.0, seed=0, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 2.0:
        return p
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    M0 = float(conv_square(p).max())
    beta = max(1e-300, float(beta_gamma) * M0)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_M = M0
    lam = float(lam0)
    stall = 0
    restarts = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    print(f"    soft_mirror [{label}] beta_gamma={beta_gamma:.3f} start_M={M0:.10g}")

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        F, M, w, q = soft_values(p, beta, True)
        if M < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M
            best_p = p.copy()

        if M > best_M * 1.12 and stall > 25:
            p = best_p.copy()
            lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            continue

        g = weighted_gradient(p, w)
        gc = g - g.mean()
        sc = float(gc.max() - gc.min())
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc < 1e-14:
            restarts += 1
            if restarts > 35:
                break
            sigma = 0.03 + 0.008 * min(restarts, 15)
            p = perturb_lowfreq(p, sigma, 3 + (restarts % 6), rng)
            lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            stall = 0
            continue

        g_unit = gc / sc
        accepted = False
        lam_list = [lam]
        if lam > 5e-3:
            lam_list.append(0.5 * lam)
        if lam > 1e-3:
            lam_list.append(0.25 * lam)
        lam_list.extend([2.0 * lam, 4.0 * lam, 0.1 * lam])

        for l in lam_list:
            if l <= 1e-6:
                continue
            p2, lp2 = apply_mirror(lp, g_unit, l)
            if p2 is None:
                continue
            F2, M2, _, _ = soft_values(p2, beta, False)
            if not np.isfinite(F2):
                continue
            if F2 < F - 1e-14:
                allowed = max(M * 1.10, best_M * 1.05)
                if M2 <= allowed:
                    p = p2
                    lp = lp2
                    lam = min(3.0, max(0.01 * float(lam), float(l) * 1.08))
                    stall = 0
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if not accepted:
            lam = max(0.01, lam * 0.62)
            stall += 1
            if stall % 25 == 0:
                restarts += 1
                sigma = 0.02 + 0.005 * min(restarts, 12)
                p = perturb_lowfreq(p, sigma, 2 + (restarts % 5), rng)
                lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                Mnow = float(conv_square(p).max())
                if Mnow < best_M - 1e-16:
                    best_M = Mnow
                    best_p = p.copy()
                if Mnow > best_M * 1.10:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                stall = 0
            if stall > 160 or restarts > 35:
                break

    if best_M >= float(conv_square(best_p).max()) - 1e-12:
        return best_p
    return best_p


def active_mirror(p0, gamma=0.03, lam0=0.1, time_limit=60.0, seed=0, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 2.0:
        return p
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    q = conv_square(p)
    M = float(q.max())
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_M = M
    lam = float(lam0)
    stall = 0
    restarts = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    print(f"    active_mirror [{label}] gamma={gamma:.4f} start_M={M:.10g}")

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        q = conv_square(p)
        M = float(q.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-16:
            best_M = M
            best_p = p.copy()

        beta = max(1e-300, float(gamma) * M)
        z = (q - M) / beta
        e = np.exp(z)
        Z = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 1e-300:
            e = np.zeros_like(q, dtype=np.float64)
            e[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
            Z = 1.0
        w = e / Z

        g = weighted_gradient(p, w)
        gc = g - g.mean()
        sc = float(gc.max() - gc.min())
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc < 1e-14:
            restarts += 1
            if restarts > 30:
                break
            sigma = 0.02 + 0.005 * min(restarts, 10)
            p = perturb_lowfreq(p, sigma, 2 + (restarts % 5), rng)
            lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
            stall = 0
            continue

        g_unit = gc / sc
        accepted = False
        lam_list = [lam, 0.5 * lam, 2.0 * lam, 0.25 * lam, 4.0 * lam, 0.1 * lam]
        for l in lam_list:
            if l <= 1e-6:
                continue
            p2, lp2 = apply_mirror(lp, g_unit, l)
            if p2 is None:
                continue
            M2 = float(conv_square(p2).max())
            if M2 < M - 1e-16:
                p = p2
                lp = lp2
                lam = min(2.5, max(0.005, float(l) * 1.03))
                stall = 0
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            lam = max(0.005, lam * 0.65)
            stall += 1
            if stall % 35 == 0:
                restarts += 1
                sigma = 0.015 + 0.005 * min(restarts, 10)
                p = perturb_lowfreq(p, sigma, 2 + (restarts % 4), rng)
                lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                Mnow = float(conv_square(p).max())
                if Mnow < best_M - 1e-16:
                    best_M = Mnow
                    best_p = p.copy()
                if Mnow > best_M * 1.08:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    lp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                stall = 0
            if stall > 180 or restarts > 30:
                break

    return best_p


def trial_transfer(p, q, s_idx, d_idx, alpha):
    n = int(p.size)
    q2 = q.copy()
    two_alpha = 2.0 * float(alpha)
    q2[d_idx : d_idx + n] += two_alpha * p
    q2[s_idx : s_idx + n] -= two_alpha * p
    aa = float(alpha) * float(alpha)
    q2[2 * d_idx] += aa
    q2[s_idx + d_idx] -= 2.0 * aa
    q2[2 * s_idx] += aa
    return q2


def coord_polish(p0, time_limit=60.0, seed=0, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 2 or time_limit <= 2.0:
        return p
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    q = conv_square(p)
    M = float(q.max())
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_M = M
    stall = 0
    restarts = 0
    step_count = 0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    top_peaks = min(72, L)
    fracs = (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 0.85)

    print(f"    coord_polish [{label}] start_M={M:.10g}")

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        if step_count > 0 and step_count % 60 == 0:
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 1e-300:
                p = p / s
            q = conv_square(p)
            M = float(q.max())
            if M < best_M - 1e-16:
                best_M = M
                best_p = p.copy()

        order = np.argsort(q)[::-1][:top_peaks]
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[order] = 1.0 / float(len(order))
        h = 0.5 * weighted_gradient(p, w)
        np.maximum(h, 0.0, out=h)

        srcs = np.argsort(h)[-4:][::-1]
        dsts = np.argsort(h)[:4]
        improved = False

        for s_i in srcs:
            s_i = int(s_i)
            ps = float(p[s_i])
            if ps <= 1e-16:
                continue
            for d_i in dsts:
                d_i = int(d_i)
                if d_i == s_i:
                    continue
                if float(h[s_i]) <= float(h[d_i]) + 1e-15:
                    continue
                best_local_M = M
                best_local_alpha = None
                best_local_q2 = None
                for fr in fracs:
                    alpha = float(fr) * ps
                    if alpha <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    q2 = trial_transfer(p, q, s_i, d_i, alpha)
                    M2 = float(q2.max())
                    if M2 < best_local_M - 1e-16:
                        best_local_M = M2
                        best_local_alpha = alpha
                        best_local_q2 = q2
                if best_local_alpha is not None and best_local_M < M - 1e-16:
                    p2 = p.copy()
                    p2[s_i] -= best_local_alpha
                    p2[d_i] += best_local_alpha
                    if float(p2.min()) < -1e-12:
                        continue
                    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
                    p = p2
                    q = best_local_q2
                    M = best_local_M
                    if M < best_M - 1e-16:
                        best_M = M
                        best_p = p.copy()
                    improved = True
                    step_count += 1
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            # occasional random coordinate push
            if stall % 6 == 0:
                s_i = int(rng.integers(n))
                d_i = int(rng.integers(n))
                if s_i != d_i and p[s_i] > 1e-14 and h[s_i] > h[d_i] + 1e-15:
                    alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.01, 0.06)) * float(p[s_i])
                    q2 = trial_transfer(p, q, s_i, d_i, alpha)
                    M2 = float(q2.max())
                    if M2 < M - 1e-16:
                        p2 = p.copy()
                        p2[s_i] -= alpha
                        p2[d_i] += alpha
                        p = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
                        q = q2
                        M = M2
                        if M < best_M - 1e-16:
                            best_M = M
                            best_p = p.copy()
                        stall = 0
                        step_count += 1

            if stall > 120:
                restarts += 1
                sigma = 0.01 + 0.004 * min(restarts, 12)
                p = perturb_lowfreq(p, sigma, 2 + (restarts % 4), rng)
                q = conv_square(p)
                M = float(q.max())
                if M < best_M - 1e-16:
                    best_M = M
                    best_p = p.copy()
                if M > best_M * 1.06:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    q = conv_square(p)
                    M = float(q.max())
                stall = 0
                if restarts > 25:
                    break

    return best_p


def interpolate_seq(p, new_n):
    new_n = int(new_n)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    old_n = int(p.size)
    if old_n == 0 or new_n <= 0:
        return make_central_p(new_n)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return normalize(p)
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(new_n, float(p[0]) / new_n)
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n, dtype=np.float64)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.interp(new_x, old_x, p)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return make_central_p(new_n)
    return a / s


def optimize_p_robust(p0, time_limit=60.0, seed=0, label=""):
    p = normalize(p0)
    if time_limit <= 5.0:
        return p
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = score_normalized(p)

    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 20.0:
        t1 = min(rem * 0.38, 320.0)
        p1 = soft_smooth_mirror(p, beta_gamma=0.6, lam0=0.35, time_limit=t1, seed=seed + 1, label=label + ":soft0")
        sc1 = score_normalized(p1)
        if sc1 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc, best_p = sc1, p1.copy()

    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 20.0:
        p = best_p.copy()
        t2 = min(rem * 0.38, 240.0)
        p2 = soft_smooth_mirror(p, beta_gamma=0.10, lam0=0.25, time_limit=t2, seed=seed + 2, label=label + ":soft1")
        sc2 = score_normalized(p2)
        if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc, best_p = sc2, p2.copy()

    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 15.0:
        p = best_p.copy()
        t3 = min(rem * 0.35, 220.0)
        p3 = active_mirror(p, gamma=0.045, lam0=0.12, time_limit=t3, seed=seed + 3, label=label + ":act")
        sc3 = score_normalized(p3)
        if sc3 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc, best_p = sc3, p3.copy()

    rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 10.0:
        p = best_p.copy()
        t4 = max(0.0, min(rem * 0.55, 420.0))
        p4 = coord_polish(p, time_limit=t4, seed=seed + 4, label=label + ":coord")
        sc4 = score_normalized(p4)
        if sc4 < best_sc - 1e-14:
            best_sc, best_p = sc4, p4.copy()

    return best_p


def make_initials(n, parent=None):
    cands = []
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []

    cx = make_central(n)
    add_x_candidate(cands, cx)

    s0 = float(cx.sum())
    pcx = cx / s0
    add_x_candidate(cands, pcx)

    tn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    for t in (-0.8, -0.4, -0.2, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8, 1.2):
        f = 1.0 + t * tn
        if np.min(f) >= -1e-9:
            add_x_candidate(cands, cx * np.maximum(f, 0.0))
        add_x_candidate(cands, cx * np.exp(0.5 * t * tn))

    for a in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        add_x_candidate(cands, cx / (1.0 + a * tn))
        add_x_candidate(cands, cx * (1.0 + a * tn))

    rev = cx[::-1]
    for a in (0.05, 0.10, 0.25, 0.50, 0.80, 1.20, 2.00):
        add_x_candidate(cands, cx + a * rev)

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        base = 2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * tn
        for d in (0.10, 0.25, 0.50):
            m1 = 1.0 + d * np.sin(base)
            if np.min(m1) >= -1e-9:
                add_x_candidate(cands, cx * m1)
            m2 = 1.0 - d * np.sin(base)
            if np.min(m2) >= -1e-9:
                add_x_candidate(cands, cx * m2)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240501)
    for _ in range(130):
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.45))
        modes = int(rng.integers(2, 9))
        pr = perturb_lowfreq(pcx, sigma, modes, rng)
        add_x_candidate(cands, pr)

    if parent is not None:
        add_x_candidate(cands, parent)

    cands.sort()
    uniq = []
    seen = set()
    for sc, _idx, p in cands:
        sig = make_signature(p)
        if sig in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(sig)
        uniq.append((float(sc), p))
        if len(uniq) >= 18:
            break
    return uniq


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    global _BEST
    _BEST = {"seq": None, "score": float("inf")}

    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 1800.0
    try:
        total_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 1800.0
    t_global = time.monotonic()
    time_budget = min(max(total_budget - 35.0, 120.0), 1770.0)

    parent = None
    par = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if par is not None:
        try:
            par = np.asarray(par, dtype=np.float64)
            par = np.where(np.isfinite(par), par, 0.0)
            par = np.maximum(par, 0.0)
            if par.ndim == 1 and par.size > 0 and float(par.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = par / float(par.sum())
                scp = score_normalized(parent)
                print(f"Parent: n={len(parent)}, fast_score={scp:.8f}")
                try_update(parent, "parent")
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if _BEST["seq"] is None:
        n_default = 4700
        if parent is not None:
            n_default = int(parent.size)
        p_default = make_central_p(n_default)
        try_update(p_default, "default_central")

    n0 = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else int(len(_BEST["seq"]))
    n0 = max(500, min(n0, 12000))
    print(f"Search base length n0={n0}, current fast_best={ _BEST['score']:.8f}")

    inits = make_initials(n0, parent)
    raw = []
    if parent is not None:
        raw.append((score_normalized(parent), len(raw), parent))
    for sc, p in inits:
        raw.append((sc, len(raw), p))
    raw.sort()
    cands = []
    seen = set()
    for sc, _idx, p in raw:
        sig = make_signature(p)
        if sig in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(sig)
        cands.append((float(sc), p))
        if len(cands) >= 8:
            break

    if not cands:
        p = make_central_p(n0)
        cands = [(score_normalized(p), p)]

    main_times = [700.0, 430.0, 300.0, 210.0, 130.0]
    for idx, (sc, p) in enumerate(cands):
        rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        if rem < 60.0:
            break
        t_alloc = min(main_times[idx] if idx < len(main_times) else 90.0, rem * 0.48)
        if t_alloc < 30.0:
            break
        print(f"\n  Optimizing candidate {idx}: initial_score={sc:.8f}, time={t_alloc:.1f}s")
        popt = optimize_p_robust(p, time_limit=t_alloc, seed=1000 + 7 * idx, label=f"init{idx}")
        try_update(popt, f"opt_init{idx}")

    # Moderate multi-length search: keep similar score target by interpolation
    # or a fresh central initializer.
    best_seq = _BEST["seq"]
    if best_seq is not None:
        n_best = int(len(best_seq))
        deltas = (-1200, -600, 600, 1200, 2400)
        new_ns = []
        for d in deltas:
            nn = n_best + int(d)
            if 1800 <= nn <= 12000:
                new_ns.append(nn)
        # Remove duplicates and keep closest to current best first.
        new_ns = sorted(set(new_ns), key=lambda x: abs(x - n_best))
        # lambda not allowed: use sorted with key via helper? Use simple two-pass.
        scored_ns = [(abs(nn - n_best), nn) for nn in new_ns]
        scored_ns.sort()
        new_ns = [nn for _d, nn in scored_ns][:3]

        for nn in new_ns:
            rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
            if rem < 90.0:
                break
            p_int = interpolate_seq(best_seq, nn)
            p_cent = make_central_p(nn)
            sc_int = score_normalized(p_int)
            sc_cent = score_normalized(p_cent)
            if sc_int <= sc_cent:
                p_try, sc_try = p_int, sc_int
            else:
                p_try, sc_try = p_cent, sc_cent
            if sc_try > _BEST["score"] * 1.05 + 1e-6:
                print(f"  Skip n={nn}, quick score {sc_try:.8f} is not promising.")
                continue
            t_alloc = min(240.0, rem * 0.30)
            if t_alloc < 40.0:
                continue
            print(f"\n  Multi-length n={nn}, quick score={sc_try:.8f}, time={t_alloc:.1f}s")
            popt = optimize_p_robust(p_try, time_limit=t_alloc, seed=7000 + (nn % 1000), label=f"n{nn}")
            try_update(popt, f"n{nn}")

    # Final polish on global best.
    rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if _BEST["seq"] is not None and rem > 35.0:
        p_best = _BEST["seq"]
        t1 = min(rem * 0.40, 220.0)
        if t1 > 20.0:
            p = active_mirror(p_best, gamma=0.025, lam0=0.07, time_limit=t1, seed=99, label="final_active")
            try_update(p, "final_active")
        rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
        t2 = min(rem * 0.55, 300.0)
        if t2 > 20.0:
            p = coord_polish(_BEST["seq"], time_limit=t2, seed=31337, label="final_coord")
            try_update(p, "final_coord")

    # One last very short soft run if there is time.
    rem = time_budget - (time.monotonic() - t_global)
    if _BEST["seq"] is not None and rem > 30.0:
        p = soft_smooth_mirror(_BEST["seq"], beta_gamma=0.05, lam0=0.1,
                               time_limit=min(rem * 0.6, 180.0), seed=555, label="final_soft")
        try_update(p, "final_soft")

    if _BEST["seq"] is None:
        p = make_central_p(4700)
        _BEST["seq"] = p
        _BEST["score"] = score_normalized(p)

    seq_list = [float(v) for v in _BEST["seq"]]
    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence(seq_list)
        print(f"FINAL fast={_BEST['score']:.8f} official={official:.8f} n={len(seq_list)} t={time.monotonic()-t_global:.1f}s")
    except Exception:
        print(f"FINAL fast={_BEST['score']:.8f} official=unavailable n={len(seq_list)} t={time.monotonic()-t_global:.1f}s")

    return seq_list
```
